Model before rewriting:

letting integerDomain be domain int(-4..3)
find a: integerDomain
find b: int(-3..3)
find p: int(-12..12)
find quotient: int(-4..4)

such that

b != 0,
p = product([a,b;int(1..)]),
quotient = (a / b)

--

b
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
b
new variables:
  find b#sign_magnitude_1: bool
  find b#sign_magnitude_2: bool
  find b#sign_magnitude_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(b#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(b#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  (b#sign_magnitude_3) -> (or([!(b#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(b#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (!(b#sign_magnitude_3)) -> (or([!(b#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(b#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (b#sign_magnitude_3) -> (or([b#sign_magnitude_1,b#sign_magnitude_2;int(1..)]))
  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [b#sign_magnitude_1,b#sign_magnitude_2,b#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)] [-3, 3]) >= -3,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [b#sign_magnitude_1,b#sign_magnitude_2,b#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)] [-3, 3]) <= 3;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
p
new variables:
  find p#sign_magnitude_1: bool
  find p#sign_magnitude_2: bool

  find p#sign_magnitude_5: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(p#sign_magnitude_4),or([!(p#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(p#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(p#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  (p#sign_magnitude_5) -> (or([!(p#sign_magnitude_4),or([!(p#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(p#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(p#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (!(p#sign_magnitude_5)) -> (or([!(p#sign_magnitude_4),or([!(p#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(p#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(p#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (p#sign_magnitude_5) -> (or([p#sign_magnitude_1,p#sign_magnitude_2,p#sign_magnitude_3,p#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)]))
  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [p#sign_magnitude_1,p#sign_magnitude_2,p#sign_magnitude_3,p#sign_magnitude_4,p#sign_magnitude_5;int(1..)] [-12, 12]) >= -12,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [p#sign_magnitude_1,p#sign_magnitude_2,p#sign_magnitude_3,p#sign_magnitude_4,p#sign_magnitude_5;int(1..)] [-12, 12]) <= 12;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
